# 1996 — every flow · minute · incomplete · complete · trap

**Status:** RESEARCH LOCK. Do not implement until this year is named as a wave.
**Feel:** portals are home · Space Jam · free webmail
**Star:** Portal wars · `itt96-portal-wars` · `sites/portals/wars.html`
**ILS June:** 257,601 · 77,433,860
**Leftover-official dests in this file:** 386
**Official 10:** frozen in `js/config/flow-trails.js`. **Guided 6:** frozen in `ui/year/start-data.js`.
**Law:** leftover never writes `itt96-portal-wars`. Trap / empty / 0 ticks / wrong pick / skip-wait never write. Neighbor-year keys stay empty.
**5k:** research envelope (ILS + Hosting.com + Pew + this dest list). Not 5,000 new rooms.

Open dests as `/years/1996/<href>`. Serve `python3 -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1`.

---

## G0 · Guided 6 (frozen · not a 7th item)

Start at `years/1996/pages/home.html` (or the year shell Starting Point).
The painted `<ol>` is **exactly 6**. A 7th `<li>` is a fail.
1. About 1996 — portals as home
2. Portal wars — Yahoo · Excite · AltaVista REAL
3. HoTMaiL — free web email
4. Space Jam — click 3 planets
5. My Yahoo! · GeoCities
6. Year flow map

Walk: About → ★ gold → official leftovers named in the list → map. Visiting a guided dest is not a write. Only the dest machine writes.

---

## G1 · ★ GOLD · `itt96-portal-wars`

**Room:** `years/1996/sites/portals/wars.html`
**Hook:** `data-portal`
**Nostalgia:** Portal wars — this is the year chip. One star.

**Incomplete never writes**
- 0 portals · trap “Google is 1996 gold” never write.
- Leftover panel on this room (if any) writes a leftover key, **not** `itt96-portal-wars`.

**Complete writes**
- Hop Yahoo + Excite + AltaVista (or portal picks) · honesty. Writes itt96-portal-wars.
- Payload is REAL (`real: true`) · year `1996` · key `itt96-portal-wars`.
- Reload still shows saved. Neighbor `itt-portal-wars` — do not write `itt95-*` or `itt97-*` gold.
- **Next after gold:** HoTMaiL → `years/1996/sites/hotmail/index.html`

**Do not:** second star · leftover save that stamps this key · dest-field plaque as the gold machine.

## L · Leftover-official — every dest · 386 flows

Protocol is the same machine (`js/immersion/leftover-official.js`). The minutes below are dest-true: key, needPick, field, next, hops.

### L-0001 · `itt96-altavista-about` · sites/altavista/about.html

**Open:** `/years/1996/sites/altavista/about.html`
**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="altavista-about"])`
**Need:** field ≥2 (placeholder “query leftover”)
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `yahoo` · `amazon` · `auctionweb`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="altavista-about"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt96-altavista-about']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
6. Ticks + pick (if any) + empty field + Save → “Empty never writes.”
7. `itt96-portal-wars` stays empty. `itt95-altavista-about` and `itt97-altavista-about` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Type ≥2 chars in `[data-lo-field]` (e.g. “query leftover”). Empty never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="altavista-about"]`.
- `localStorage['itt96-altavista-about']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"1996", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt96-altavista-about`.
- Does **not** write `itt96-portal-wars`.
- **Next:** Next leftover → `/years/1996/sites/mtv/more.html`

**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="playable-extra-c-d2"])`
**Need:** needPick=`keep`
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `yahoo` · `amazon` · `auctionweb`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="playable-extra-c-d2"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt96-playable-extra-c-d2']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
5. Ticks + wrong pick (not `keep`) + Save → “Wrong leftover.” Key absent.
7. `itt96-portal-wars` stays empty. `itt95-playable-extra-c-d2` and `itt97-playable-extra-c-d2` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Click `[data-lo-pick="keep"]`. Wrong pick never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="playable-extra-c-d2"]`.
- `localStorage['itt96-playable-extra-c-d2']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"1996", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt96-playable-extra-c-d2`.
- Does **not** write `itt96-portal-wars`.
- **Next:** Real buffer → `/years/1996/sites/playable/game-3.html`
